What just happened

In the first run, the first model call failed twice and worked on the third attempt, and the rest was the normal fix. The summary counts the 2 retries. In the second run, every attempt failed. Each call made three attempts, and each failed call moved the breaker one step. After three failed calls the breaker opened, and Rein stopped with a clear message instead of retrying forever.

Talking point: "I switched off the SDK's built-in retries so the harness owns one retry policy that is counted, traced, and limited, with a circuit breaker so an outage cannot become an endless loop."

Break it on purpose

  1. In with_retries(), change attempts=3 to attempts=1. There are no retries now: every failure goes straight to the breaker.
  2. Make the FakeModel raise ValueError instead of TransientError, and run the first checkpoint. Rein crashes with a traceback. That is the right behavior: a bug is not worth retrying, and hiding it would only make it harder to find. Retry only what can succeed if you wait. Put it back.
  3. Why does jitter matter much more for a service with a million users than for you alone?
The whole rein.py after step 7 (454 lines)
"""Rein: a small coding-agent harness, built one step at a time.

Each section below is one system of the harness. Read it from top to bottom.
"""
import argparse
import json
import os
import random
import re
import subprocess
import time
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path


def show(tag, text):
    """Print one line of the live transcript: line breaks become spaces, and it is cut to fit."""
    print(f"{tag:<8} {' '.join(str(text).splitlines())}"[:100])


def one_line(text):
    """Join the lines of a tool result with ' | ' for the transcript. The model still gets all of it."""
    return " | ".join(line.strip() for line in str(text).splitlines() if line.strip())


# ============ MODEL: one interface, two models ============

class TransientError(Exception):
    """A failure worth retrying: overloaded, rate-limited, or a network blip."""


@dataclass
class ToolCall:
    id: str      # every request gets an id, so its result can be matched to it
    name: str    # which tool the model wants
    input: dict  # the arguments the model chose


@dataclass
class Reply:
    text: str          # what the model wrote
    tool_calls: list   # the tools it asked for (maybe none)
    stop_reason: str   # why it stopped: "end_turn", "tool_use", "max_tokens", ...
    content: list      # the raw blocks, saved into the history exactly as they came
    usage: dict = field(default_factory=dict)  # input and output token counts


class FakeModel:
    """Replays a script of replies, so every run is free, offline, and repeatable."""

    def __init__(self, replies):
        self.replies = list(replies)
        self.calls = 0

    def call(self, system, messages, tools):
        step = self.replies.pop(0) if self.replies else {"say": "(the script has run out)"}
        if "error" in step:            # a script can pretend the API failed
            if step.get("forever"):     # an outage that never ends
                self.replies.insert(0, step)
            raise TransientError(step["error"])
        content, tool_calls = [], []
        if step.get("say"):
            content.append({"type": "text", "text": step["say"]})
        for name, args in step.get("tools", []):
            self.calls += 1
            call = ToolCall(f"call_{self.calls}", name, args)
            tool_calls.append(call)
            content.append({"type": "tool_use", "id": call.id, "name": name, "input": args})
        stop = step.get("stop") or ("tool_use" if tool_calls else "end_turn")
        usage = {"input_tokens": len(json.dumps(messages)) // 4,
                 "output_tokens": len(json.dumps(content)) // 4}
        return Reply(step.get("say", ""), tool_calls, stop, content, usage)


class ClaudeModel:
    """The real model, behind exactly the same interface."""

    def __init__(self, model=None):
        import anthropic
        self.client = anthropic.Anthropic(max_retries=0)  # the harness owns retries now
        self.model = model or os.environ.get("REIN_MODEL", "claude-sonnet-5")
        names = ("RateLimitError", "APIConnectionError", "InternalServerError", "OverloadedError")
        self.retryable = tuple(getattr(anthropic, n) for n in names if hasattr(anthropic, n))

    def call(self, system, messages, tools):
        extra = {"tools": tools} if tools else {}
        try:
            r = self.client.messages.create(model=self.model, max_tokens=4096,
                                            system=system, messages=messages, **extra)
        except self.retryable as e:
            raise TransientError(type(e).__name__) from e
        content = [block.model_dump(exclude_none=True) for block in r.content]
        return Reply(
            text="".join(b["text"] for b in content if b["type"] == "text"),
            tool_calls=[ToolCall(b["id"], b["name"], b["input"])
                        for b in content if b["type"] == "tool_use"],
            stop_reason=r.stop_reason,
            content=content,
            usage={"input_tokens": r.usage.input_tokens,
                   "output_tokens": r.usage.output_tokens},
        )


# ============ TOOLS: the model's hands ============

class ToolError(Exception):
    """An expected failure. Its message goes back to the model, so write it for the model."""


class Workspace:
    """Every path goes through here, so the agent cannot leave its project folder."""

    def __init__(self, root):
        self.root = Path(root).resolve()

    def path(self, rel):
        p = (self.root / rel).resolve()
        if p != self.root and self.root not in p.parents:
            raise ToolError(f"'{rel}' is outside the project. Use a path inside it.")
        return p


@dataclass
class Tool:
    name: str
    description: str         # the model reads this, so write it for the model
    schema: dict             # the inputs, described as JSON Schema
    run: object              # the Python function the harness calls
    read_only: bool = False  # True if the tool never changes anything

    def spec(self):
        return {"name": self.name, "description": self.description, "input_schema": self.schema}


# Commands run without writing .pyc caches, so a check never runs stale code.
CLEAN_ENV = {**os.environ, "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE": "1"}


def make_tools(ws):
    """Four tools. Every error message tells the model what to do next."""

    def list_files():
        files = [p.relative_to(ws.root).as_posix() for p in sorted(ws.root.rglob("*")) if p.is_file()]
        return "\n".join(f for f in files if "__pycache__" not in f) or "(no files)"

    def read_file(path, start_line=1):
        p = ws.path(path)
        if not p.is_file():
            raise ToolError(f"No file at '{path}'. Call list_files to see what exists.")
        lines = p.read_text().splitlines()
        page = lines[start_line - 1:start_line + 199]
        text = "\n".join(f"{n}| {line}" for n, line in enumerate(page, start_line))
        last = start_line + len(page) - 1
        if last < len(lines):
            text += f"\n[lines {start_line}-{last} of {len(lines)}. Call again with start_line={last + 1}.]"
        return text or "(empty file)"

    def edit_file(path, old_text, new_text):
        p = ws.path(path)
        if not p.is_file():
            raise ToolError(f"No file at '{path}'. Call list_files to see what exists.")
        text = p.read_text()
        count = text.count(old_text)
        if count == 0:
            raise ToolError("old_text was not found. Read the file again: spaces must match exactly.")
        if count > 1:
            raise ToolError(f"old_text appears {count} times. Include more lines so it is unique.")
        p.write_text(text.replace(old_text, new_text))
        return f"Edited {path}."

    def run_command(command):
        try:
            r = subprocess.run(command, shell=True, cwd=ws.root, capture_output=True,
                               text=True, timeout=60, env=CLEAN_ENV)
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
            raise ToolError("Timed out after 60 seconds. Try a smaller command.")
        return f"exit code {r.returncode}\n{(r.stdout + r.stderr).strip() or '(no output)'}"

    string = {"type": "string"}
    tools = [
        Tool("list_files", "List every file in the project. Start here.",
             {"type": "object", "properties": {}}, list_files, read_only=True),
        Tool("read_file", "Read a text file with line numbers, 200 lines per page.",
             {"type": "object", "properties": {"path": string, "start_line": {"type": "integer"}},
              "required": ["path"]}, read_file, read_only=True),
        Tool("edit_file", "Replace one exact, unique piece of text in a file. Read the file first.",
             {"type": "object", "properties": {"path": string, "old_text": string, "new_text": string},
              "required": ["path", "old_text", "new_text"]}, edit_file),
        Tool("run_command", "Run a shell command in the project folder, for example the tests.",
             {"type": "object", "properties": {"command": string}, "required": ["command"]},
             run_command),
    ]
    return {t.name: t for t in tools}


# ============ PERMISSIONS: allow, ask, or deny ============

BLOCKED = [r"\brm\s", r"\bsudo\b", r"\bgit\s+push\b", r"\bcurl\b", r"\bwget\b"]  # never
ALLOWED = [r"^python\s+test_\w+\.py\b", r"^python\s+-m\s+pytest\b"]            # always fine
CHAINING = re.compile(r"[;&|`$<>]")  # an allowed command must not smuggle in a second one


@dataclass
class Verdict:
    decision: str  # "allow", "ask", or "deny"
    reason: str    # the model reads this too, so say why


def decide(tool, args, mode):
    """The policy. Deny rules are checked before allow rules."""
    if tool.read_only:
        return Verdict("allow", "read-only tool")
    if tool.name == "run_command":
        command = args.get("command", "").strip()
        if any(re.search(p, command) for p in BLOCKED):
            return Verdict("deny", "destructive, network, or admin command")
        if any(re.search(p, command) for p in ALLOWED) and not CHAINING.search(command):
            return Verdict("allow", "allow-listed command")
        return Verdict("ask", "this command is not on the allow-list")
    if mode == "auto":
        return Verdict("allow", "edits are allowed in auto mode")
    return Verdict("ask", "this tool changes files")


def ask_human(call, reason):
    answer = input(f"         ? allow {call.name}? ({reason}) [y/N] ")
    return answer.strip().lower() == "y"


# ============ TRACE: a record of everything ============

class Tracer:
    """Writes one JSON object per event to a file (JSONL), and keeps them for a summary."""

    def __init__(self, path):
        self.path, self.start, self.events = path, time.time(), []
        Path(path).write_text("")  # a fresh trace file for every run

    def event(self, kind, **data):
        record = {"t": round(time.time() - self.start, 3), "kind": kind, **data}
        self.events.append(record)
        with open(self.path, "a") as f:
            f.write(json.dumps(record) + "\n")

    def summary(self):
        counts = {}
        for e in self.events:
            if e["kind"] not in ("run_start", "run_end"):
                counts[e["kind"]] = counts.get(e["kind"], 0) + 1
        tokens = sum(e.get("input_tokens", 0) + e.get("output_tokens", 0) for e in self.events)
        return ", ".join(f"{n} {kind}" for kind, n in counts.items()) + f", {tokens} tokens"


# ============ CONTEXT: keep the desk clear ============

def estimate_tokens(messages):
    """About 4 characters per token. Rough, but good enough to decide when to compact."""
    return len(json.dumps(messages)) // 4


def truncate(text, max_chars):
    """Keep the head and the tail. In logs, the error is usually near the end."""
    if len(text) <= max_chars:
        return text
    half = max_chars // 2
    return f"{text[:half]}\n[... {len(text) - max_chars} characters cut by the harness ...]\n{text[-half:]}"


def compact(messages, task, keep_last=4):
    """Swap old history for a short summary. Keep the newest messages word for word."""
    cut = len(messages) - keep_last
    while cut > 1 and messages[cut]["role"] != "assistant":  # never split a tool call from its result
        cut -= 1
    if cut <= 1:
        return None  # nothing old enough to summarize yet
    used = [b["name"] for m in messages[:cut] if isinstance(m["content"], list)
            for b in m["content"] if b.get("type") == "tool_use"]
    summary = ("[The harness summarized earlier work to save space.]\n"
               f"Original task: {task}\n"
               f"Tools used so far: {', '.join(used) or 'none'}\n"
               "Re-read any file before you edit it. Then continue the task.")
    return [{"role": "user", "content": summary}] + messages[cut:]


# ============ RECOVERY: counted, limited, able to give up ============

def with_retries(call, on_retry, attempts=3, base=0.5, cap=8.0):
    """Try again after a transient failure, waiting longer each time."""
    for attempt in range(1, attempts + 1):
        try:
            return call()
        except TransientError as e:
            if attempt == attempts:
                raise                                    # out of attempts: the caller decides
            delay = min(cap, base * 2 ** (attempt - 1))  # 0.5s, 1s, 2s, 4s... never above cap
            on_retry(attempt, delay, e)
            time.sleep(delay * random.uniform(0.5, 1.0))  # jitter: never retry in lockstep


class CircuitBreaker:
    """After `threshold` failed calls in a row, stop trying and say so clearly."""

    def __init__(self, threshold=3):
        self.threshold, self.failures = threshold, 0

    def record(self, ok):
        self.failures = 0 if ok else self.failures + 1

    @property
    def is_open(self):
        return self.failures >= self.threshold


# ============ THE LOOP ============

SYSTEM_PROMPT = ("You are Rein, a careful coding agent working inside one project folder. "
                 "Look before you change anything, and run the tests to prove your work.")


@dataclass
class Config:
    max_turns: int = 10  # hard stop: the most model calls one run may use
    workspace: str = "demo"  # the only folder the agent may touch
    mode: str = "ask"        # "ask": edits need a human yes. "auto": edits are allowed
    trace_file: str = "trace.jsonl"  # every event of the run is recorded here
    tool_output_chars: int = 2000    # cap on each tool result that enters the context
    context_limit: int = 20000       # estimated tokens before old history is compacted


class Agent:
    def __init__(self, model, cfg, trace):
        self.model, self.cfg, self.trace = model, cfg, trace
        self.tools = make_tools(Workspace(cfg.workspace))
        self.specs = [t.spec() for t in self.tools.values()]
        self.denied = set()  # once denied, always denied
        self.breaker = CircuitBreaker()

    def say(self, tag, text):
        show(tag, text)

    def finish(self, status, note, answer=""):
        """Every way a run can end goes through here, so none of them gets forgotten."""
        self.say(status, note)
        self.trace.event("run_end", status=status)
        return answer

    def on_retry(self, attempt, delay, error):
        self.say("retry", f"attempt {attempt} failed ({error}); waiting up to {delay:.1f}s")
        self.trace.event("retry", attempt=attempt, error=str(error))

    def run(self, task):
        messages = [{"role": "user", "content": task}]
        self.say("task", task)
        self.trace.event("run_start", task=task)
        for turn in range(1, self.cfg.max_turns + 1):
            size = estimate_tokens(messages)
            smaller = compact(messages, task) if size > self.cfg.context_limit else None
            if smaller:
                messages[:] = smaller  # change the list in place, so every holder sees it
                self.say("compact", f"~{size} tokens is over the limit ({self.cfg.context_limit}); "
                                    f"now ~{estimate_tokens(messages)}")
                self.trace.event("compact", before=size, after=estimate_tokens(messages))
            try:
                reply = with_retries(lambda: self.model.call(SYSTEM_PROMPT, messages, self.specs),
                                     on_retry=self.on_retry)
                self.breaker.record(ok=True)
            except TransientError as e:
                self.breaker.record(ok=False)
                self.say("error", f"model call failed ({e}); breaker at "
                                  f"{self.breaker.failures} of {self.breaker.threshold}")
                self.trace.event("model_error", error=str(e))
                if self.breaker.is_open:
                    return self.finish("stopped", "the model API keeps failing; the circuit breaker is open")
                continue
            self.trace.event("model_call", turn=turn, stop_reason=reply.stop_reason, **reply.usage)
            messages.append({"role": "assistant", "content": reply.content})
            self.say(f"turn {turn}", f"model: {reply.text}" if reply.text else "model asks for tools")
            if not reply.tool_calls:
                return self.finish("done", f"after {turn} model calls", reply.text)
            results = []
            for call in reply.tool_calls:
                self.say("", f"tool   {call.name} {json.dumps(call.input)}")
                output, is_error = self.run_tool(call)
                self.trace.event("tool_call", tool=call.name, error=is_error, chars=len(output))
                self.say("", ("error  " if is_error else "result ") + one_line(output))
                results.append({"type": "tool_result", "tool_use_id": call.id,
                                "content": output, "is_error": is_error})
            messages.append({"role": "user", "content": results})
        return self.finish("stopped", f"turn limit reached ({self.cfg.max_turns} model calls)")

    def run_tool(self, call):
        """Run one tool call. Any error becomes a message for the model, never a crash."""
        tool = self.tools.get(call.name)
        if tool is None:
            return f"Unknown tool '{call.name}'. Available tools: {', '.join(self.tools)}.", True
        verdict = self.check_permission(tool, call)
        self.say("", f"{verdict.decision:<6} {verdict.reason}")
        if verdict.decision == "deny":
            return f"Permission denied: {verdict.reason}. Do not retry this; find another way.", True
        try:
            output = tool.run(**call.input)
        except ToolError as e:
            return str(e), True
        except Exception as e:  # a bug, or an argument the model made up
            return f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}", True
        if len(output) > self.cfg.tool_output_chars:
            self.say("", f"cut    {len(output)} characters down to {self.cfg.tool_output_chars}")
        return truncate(output, self.cfg.tool_output_chars), False

    def check_permission(self, tool, call):
        key = call.name + json.dumps(call.input, sort_keys=True)
        if key in self.denied:
            verdict = Verdict("deny", "this exact call was already denied")
        else:
            verdict = decide(tool, call.input, self.cfg.mode)
        if verdict.decision == "ask":
            yes = ask_human(call, verdict.reason)
            verdict = Verdict("allow", "a human said yes") if yes else Verdict("deny", "a human said no")
        if verdict.decision == "deny":
            self.denied.add(key)
        self.trace.event("permission", tool=call.name, decision=verdict.decision, reason=verdict.reason)
        return verdict


# ============ COMMAND LINE ============

def main():
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Rein: a small coding-agent harness")
    p.add_argument("task", help="what you want done, in plain words")
    p.add_argument("--fake", metavar="SCENARIO", help="use a scripted model from this JSON file")
    p.add_argument("--workspace", default="demo", help="the project folder the agent works in")
    p.add_argument("--mode", default="ask", choices=["ask", "auto"], help="auto: allow edits without asking")
    p.add_argument("--context-limit", type=int, default=20000, help="compact above this many tokens")
    args = p.parse_args()

    if args.fake:
        with open(args.fake) as f:
            scenario = json.load(f)
        model = FakeModel(scenario["replies"])
    else:
        model = ClaudeModel()

    cfg = Config(
        workspace=args.workspace,
        mode=args.mode,
        context_limit=args.context_limit,
    )
    trace = Tracer(cfg.trace_file)
    Agent(model, cfg, trace).run(args.task)
    show("trace", f"{trace.summary()} (details in {cfg.trace_file})")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

8 Stopping well

GoalFour more guards: a loop detector, handling for cut-off replies, a token budget, and a clean stop on Ctrl+C. TheoryChapter 10: loop state and stopping well Sizerein.py grows to 499 lines

8aTwo recovery helpers

LoopDetector remembers the last tool call, and counts how many times in a row the exact same call comes in. Three in a row means the model is going in circles; this is Loop detection.

close_open_calls() repairs the history after an interrupt. If the last message is the model asking for tools, each request still needs a result, or the next API call will be rejected. It adds a result for each one saying it was interrupted.

class LoopDetector:    """Notices the exact same tool call, made several times in a row."""    def __init__(self, limit=3):        self.limit, self.last, self.count = limit, None, 0    def repeated(self, call):        key = call.name + json.dumps(call.input, sort_keys=True)        self.count = self.count + 1 if key == self.last else 1        self.last = key        return self.count >= self.limitdef close_open_calls(messages):    """After an interrupt, every tool request still needs a result, or the history is broken."""    last = messages[-1]    if last["role"] == "assistant":        ids = [b["id"] for b in last["content"] if b.get("type") == "tool_use"]        if ids:            messages.append({"role": "user", "content": [                {"type": "tool_result", "tool_use_id": i, "content": "Interrupted before it ran.",                 "is_error": True} for i in ids]})# ============ THE LOOP ============

8bFour guards

  • Ctrl+C. run() becomes a small wrapper around a new loop(). If you press Ctrl+C, it closes any open tool calls and stops cleanly, with a valid history, instead of dying with a traceback. This is why step 6 changed messages in place: run() and loop() share one list.
  • A token budget. The loop adds up the tokens of every call, and stops before the next call once the budget is spent.
  • Cut-off replies. A reply that stopped because it ran out of room (max_tokens) is kept, and the model is asked to continue exactly where it stopped. Continuing is cheaper and more faithful than asking for a summary.
  • The loop detector runs before the gate, and answers a repeated call with an error that tells the model to try something else.

One case is left out on purpose: a reply cut off in the middle of a tool call. Its arguments may be half written, so they cannot be trusted. The reference project in B7 drops such a reply and asks for a smaller step.

What just happened

Turns 1 and 2 read the same file. On the third identical call, the loop detector answered with an error instead of running it, and the model changed course. Turn 4 was cut off mid-word, at "subtra". Rein kept it and asked for the rest, and turn 5 carried on with "cts". In the second run, the budget was 700 tokens. After turn 4, Rein had spent 701, so it stopped before making another call.

Every guard stopped or redirected the run on the harness's terms, and each one told you why.

The scripted model is too fast to interrupt. With Claude, give Rein a longer task, like "Add a subtract function to calc.py, with a test", and press Ctrl+C while it works. You get a clean stopped line with the number of messages, instead of a traceback.

Break it on purpose

  1. Delete the four lines that start with if reply.stop_reason == "max_tokens", and run the first checkpoint. The run now ends at turn 4, with half a sentence reported as done. The harness mistook "ran out of room" for "finished". Put the lines back.
  2. Set the LoopDetector limit to 2. What happens on turn 2? Would 2 be too strict for real work? Think of a model that reads a file again after editing it.
  3. List every way a run can end now. Step 9 adds one more.

The API is overloaded for ten minutes. What should a good harness do?

  • Retry immediately, as fast as it can, until it works.
  • Retry a few times with growing, randomized waits, then stop and report clearly.
  • Crash, so someone notices.

Fast retries make an overload worse, and endless retries waste time and money. Backoff with jitter handles short hiccups; a circuit breaker turns a long outage into a clear stop.

The whole rein.py after step 8 (499 lines)
"""Rein: a small coding-agent harness, built one step at a time.

Each section below is one system of the harness. Read it from top to bottom.
"""
import argparse
import json
import os
import random
import re
import subprocess
import time
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path


def show(tag, text):
    """Print one line of the live transcript: line breaks become spaces, and it is cut to fit."""
    print(f"{tag:<8} {' '.join(str(text).splitlines())}"[:100])


def one_line(text):
    """Join the lines of a tool result with ' | ' for the transcript. The model still gets all of it."""
    return " | ".join(line.strip() for line in str(text).splitlines() if line.strip())


# ============ MODEL: one interface, two models ============

class TransientError(Exception):
    """A failure worth retrying: overloaded, rate-limited, or a network blip."""


@dataclass
class ToolCall:
    id: str      # every request gets an id, so its result can be matched to it
    name: str    # which tool the model wants
    input: dict  # the arguments the model chose


@dataclass
class Reply:
    text: str          # what the model wrote
    tool_calls: list   # the tools it asked for (maybe none)
    stop_reason: str   # why it stopped: "end_turn", "tool_use", "max_tokens", ...
    content: list      # the raw blocks, saved into the history exactly as they came
    usage: dict = field(default_factory=dict)  # input and output token counts


class FakeModel:
    """Replays a script of replies, so every run is free, offline, and repeatable."""

    def __init__(self, replies):
        self.replies = list(replies)
        self.calls = 0

    def call(self, system, messages, tools):
        step = self.replies.pop(0) if self.replies else {"say": "(the script has run out)"}
        if "error" in step:            # a script can pretend the API failed
            if step.get("forever"):     # an outage that never ends
                self.replies.insert(0, step)
            raise TransientError(step["error"])
        content, tool_calls = [], []
        if step.get("say"):
            content.append({"type": "text", "text": step["say"]})
        for name, args in step.get("tools", []):
            self.calls += 1
            call = ToolCall(f"call_{self.calls}", name, args)
            tool_calls.append(call)
            content.append({"type": "tool_use", "id": call.id, "name": name, "input": args})
        stop = step.get("stop") or ("tool_use" if tool_calls else "end_turn")
        usage = {"input_tokens": len(json.dumps(messages)) // 4,
                 "output_tokens": len(json.dumps(content)) // 4}
        return Reply(step.get("say", ""), tool_calls, stop, content, usage)


class ClaudeModel:
    """The real model, behind exactly the same interface."""

    def __init__(self, model=None):
        import anthropic
        self.client = anthropic.Anthropic(max_retries=0)  # the harness owns retries now
        self.model = model or os.environ.get("REIN_MODEL", "claude-sonnet-5")
        names = ("RateLimitError", "APIConnectionError", "InternalServerError", "OverloadedError")
        self.retryable = tuple(getattr(anthropic, n) for n in names if hasattr(anthropic, n))

    def call(self, system, messages, tools):
        extra = {"tools": tools} if tools else {}
        try:
            r = self.client.messages.create(model=self.model, max_tokens=4096,
                                            system=system, messages=messages, **extra)
        except self.retryable as e:
            raise TransientError(type(e).__name__) from e
        content = [block.model_dump(exclude_none=True) for block in r.content]
        return Reply(
            text="".join(b["text"] for b in content if b["type"] == "text"),
            tool_calls=[ToolCall(b["id"], b["name"], b["input"])
                        for b in content if b["type"] == "tool_use"],
            stop_reason=r.stop_reason,
            content=content,
            usage={"input_tokens": r.usage.input_tokens,
                   "output_tokens": r.usage.output_tokens},
        )


# ============ TOOLS: the model's hands ============

class ToolError(Exception):
    """An expected failure. Its message goes back to the model, so write it for the model."""


class Workspace:
    """Every path goes through here, so the agent cannot leave its project folder."""

    def __init__(self, root):
        self.root = Path(root).resolve()

    def path(self, rel):
        p = (self.root / rel).resolve()
        if p != self.root and self.root not in p.parents:
            raise ToolError(f"'{rel}' is outside the project. Use a path inside it.")
        return p


@dataclass
class Tool:
    name: str
    description: str         # the model reads this, so write it for the model
    schema: dict             # the inputs, described as JSON Schema
    run: object              # the Python function the harness calls
    read_only: bool = False  # True if the tool never changes anything

    def spec(self):
        return {"name": self.name, "description": self.description, "input_schema": self.schema}


# Commands run without writing .pyc caches, so a check never runs stale code.
CLEAN_ENV = {**os.environ, "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE": "1"}


def make_tools(ws):
    """Four tools. Every error message tells the model what to do next."""

    def list_files():
        files = [p.relative_to(ws.root).as_posix() for p in sorted(ws.root.rglob("*")) if p.is_file()]
        return "\n".join(f for f in files if "__pycache__" not in f) or "(no files)"

    def read_file(path, start_line=1):
        p = ws.path(path)
        if not p.is_file():
            raise ToolError(f"No file at '{path}'. Call list_files to see what exists.")
        lines = p.read_text().splitlines()
        page = lines[start_line - 1:start_line + 199]
        text = "\n".join(f"{n}| {line}" for n, line in enumerate(page, start_line))
        last = start_line + len(page) - 1
        if last < len(lines):
            text += f"\n[lines {start_line}-{last} of {len(lines)}. Call again with start_line={last + 1}.]"
        return text or "(empty file)"

    def edit_file(path, old_text, new_text):
        p = ws.path(path)
        if not p.is_file():
            raise ToolError(f"No file at '{path}'. Call list_files to see what exists.")
        text = p.read_text()
        count = text.count(old_text)
        if count == 0:
            raise ToolError("old_text was not found. Read the file again: spaces must match exactly.")
        if count > 1:
            raise ToolError(f"old_text appears {count} times. Include more lines so it is unique.")
        p.write_text(text.replace(old_text, new_text))
        return f"Edited {path}."

    def run_command(command):
        try:
            r = subprocess.run(command, shell=True, cwd=ws.root, capture_output=True,
                               text=True, timeout=60, env=CLEAN_ENV)
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
            raise ToolError("Timed out after 60 seconds. Try a smaller command.")
        return f"exit code {r.returncode}\n{(r.stdout + r.stderr).strip() or '(no output)'}"

    string = {"type": "string"}
    tools = [
        Tool("list_files", "List every file in the project. Start here.",
             {"type": "object", "properties": {}}, list_files, read_only=True),
        Tool("read_file", "Read a text file with line numbers, 200 lines per page.",
             {"type": "object", "properties": {"path": string, "start_line": {"type": "integer"}},
              "required": ["path"]}, read_file, read_only=True),
        Tool("edit_file", "Replace one exact, unique piece of text in a file. Read the file first.",
             {"type": "object", "properties": {"path": string, "old_text": string, "new_text": string},
              "required": ["path", "old_text", "new_text"]}, edit_file),
        Tool("run_command", "Run a shell command in the project folder, for example the tests.",
             {"type": "object", "properties": {"command": string}, "required": ["command"]},
             run_command),
    ]
    return {t.name: t for t in tools}


# ============ PERMISSIONS: allow, ask, or deny ============

BLOCKED = [r"\brm\s", r"\bsudo\b", r"\bgit\s+push\b", r"\bcurl\b", r"\bwget\b"]  # never
ALLOWED = [r"^python\s+test_\w+\.py\b", r"^python\s+-m\s+pytest\b"]            # always fine
CHAINING = re.compile(r"[;&|`$<>]")  # an allowed command must not smuggle in a second one


@dataclass
class Verdict:
    decision: str  # "allow", "ask", or "deny"
    reason: str    # the model reads this too, so say why


def decide(tool, args, mode):
    """The policy. Deny rules are checked before allow rules."""
    if tool.read_only:
        return Verdict("allow", "read-only tool")
    if tool.name == "run_command":
        command = args.get("command", "").strip()
        if any(re.search(p, command) for p in BLOCKED):
            return Verdict("deny", "destructive, network, or admin command")
        if any(re.search(p, command) for p in ALLOWED) and not CHAINING.search(command):
            return Verdict("allow", "allow-listed command")
        return Verdict("ask", "this command is not on the allow-list")
    if mode == "auto":
        return Verdict("allow", "edits are allowed in auto mode")
    return Verdict("ask", "this tool changes files")


def ask_human(call, reason):
    answer = input(f"         ? allow {call.name}? ({reason}) [y/N] ")
    return answer.strip().lower() == "y"


# ============ TRACE: a record of everything ============

class Tracer:
    """Writes one JSON object per event to a file (JSONL), and keeps them for a summary."""

    def __init__(self, path):
        self.path, self.start, self.events = path, time.time(), []
        Path(path).write_text("")  # a fresh trace file for every run

    def event(self, kind, **data):
        record = {"t": round(time.time() - self.start, 3), "kind": kind, **data}
        self.events.append(record)
        with open(self.path, "a") as f:
            f.write(json.dumps(record) + "\n")

    def summary(self):
        counts = {}
        for e in self.events:
            if e["kind"] not in ("run_start", "run_end"):
                counts[e["kind"]] = counts.get(e["kind"], 0) + 1
        tokens = sum(e.get("input_tokens", 0) + e.get("output_tokens", 0) for e in self.events)
        return ", ".join(f"{n} {kind}" for kind, n in counts.items()) + f", {tokens} tokens"


# ============ CONTEXT: keep the desk clear ============

def estimate_tokens(messages):
    """About 4 characters per token. Rough, but good enough to decide when to compact."""
    return len(json.dumps(messages)) // 4


def truncate(text, max_chars):
    """Keep the head and the tail. In logs, the error is usually near the end."""
    if len(text) <= max_chars:
        return text
    half = max_chars // 2
    return f"{text[:half]}\n[... {len(text) - max_chars} characters cut by the harness ...]\n{text[-half:]}"


def compact(messages, task, keep_last=4):
    """Swap old history for a short summary. Keep the newest messages word for word."""
    cut = len(messages) - keep_last
    while cut > 1 and messages[cut]["role"] != "assistant":  # never split a tool call from its result
        cut -= 1
    if cut <= 1:
        return None  # nothing old enough to summarize yet
    used = [b["name"] for m in messages[:cut] if isinstance(m["content"], list)
            for b in m["content"] if b.get("type") == "tool_use"]
    summary = ("[The harness summarized earlier work to save space.]\n"
               f"Original task: {task}\n"
               f"Tools used so far: {', '.join(used) or 'none'}\n"
               "Re-read any file before you edit it. Then continue the task.")
    return [{"role": "user", "content": summary}] + messages[cut:]


# ============ RECOVERY: counted, limited, able to give up ============

def with_retries(call, on_retry, attempts=3, base=0.5, cap=8.0):
    """Try again after a transient failure, waiting longer each time."""
    for attempt in range(1, attempts + 1):
        try:
            return call()
        except TransientError as e:
            if attempt == attempts:
                raise                                    # out of attempts: the caller decides
            delay = min(cap, base * 2 ** (attempt - 1))  # 0.5s, 1s, 2s, 4s... never above cap
            on_retry(attempt, delay, e)
            time.sleep(delay * random.uniform(0.5, 1.0))  # jitter: never retry in lockstep


class CircuitBreaker:
    """After `threshold` failed calls in a row, stop trying and say so clearly."""

    def __init__(self, threshold=3):
        self.threshold, self.failures = threshold, 0

    def record(self, ok):
        self.failures = 0 if ok else self.failures + 1

    @property
    def is_open(self):
        return self.failures >= self.threshold


class LoopDetector:
    """Notices the exact same tool call, made several times in a row."""

    def __init__(self, limit=3):
        self.limit, self.last, self.count = limit, None, 0

    def repeated(self, call):
        key = call.name + json.dumps(call.input, sort_keys=True)
        self.count = self.count + 1 if key == self.last else 1
        self.last = key
        return self.count >= self.limit


def close_open_calls(messages):
    """After an interrupt, every tool request still needs a result, or the history is broken."""
    last = messages[-1]
    if last["role"] == "assistant":
        ids = [b["id"] for b in last["content"] if b.get("type") == "tool_use"]
        if ids:
            messages.append({"role": "user", "content": [
                {"type": "tool_result", "tool_use_id": i, "content": "Interrupted before it ran.",
                 "is_error": True} for i in ids]})


# ============ THE LOOP ============

SYSTEM_PROMPT = ("You are Rein, a careful coding agent working inside one project folder. "
                 "Look before you change anything, and run the tests to prove your work.")


@dataclass
class Config:
    max_turns: int = 10  # hard stop: the most model calls one run may use
    workspace: str = "demo"  # the only folder the agent may touch
    mode: str = "ask"        # "ask": edits need a human yes. "auto": edits are allowed
    trace_file: str = "trace.jsonl"  # every event of the run is recorded here
    tool_output_chars: int = 2000    # cap on each tool result that enters the context
    context_limit: int = 20000       # estimated tokens before old history is compacted
    token_budget: int = 200000       # hard stop: the most tokens one run may spend


class Agent:
    def __init__(self, model, cfg, trace):
        self.model, self.cfg, self.trace = model, cfg, trace
        self.tools = make_tools(Workspace(cfg.workspace))
        self.specs = [t.spec() for t in self.tools.values()]
        self.denied = set()  # once denied, always denied
        self.breaker = CircuitBreaker()
        self.loops = LoopDetector()

    def say(self, tag, text):
        show(tag, text)

    def finish(self, status, note, answer=""):
        """Every way a run can end goes through here, so none of them gets forgotten."""
        self.say(status, note)
        self.trace.event("run_end", status=status)
        return answer

    def on_retry(self, attempt, delay, error):
        self.say("retry", f"attempt {attempt} failed ({error}); waiting up to {delay:.1f}s")
        self.trace.event("retry", attempt=attempt, error=str(error))

    def run(self, task):
        messages = [{"role": "user", "content": task}]
        try:
            return self.loop(task, messages)
        except KeyboardInterrupt:  # Ctrl+C: stop cleanly and leave a valid history behind
            close_open_calls(messages)
            return self.finish("stopped", f"interrupted; history left valid ({len(messages)} messages)")

    def loop(self, task, messages):
        self.say("task", task)
        self.trace.event("run_start", task=task)
        tokens = 0
        for turn in range(1, self.cfg.max_turns + 1):
            if tokens >= self.cfg.token_budget:
                return self.finish("stopped", f"token budget spent ({tokens} of {self.cfg.token_budget})")
            size = estimate_tokens(messages)
            smaller = compact(messages, task) if size > self.cfg.context_limit else None
            if smaller:
                messages[:] = smaller  # change the list in place, so every holder sees it
                self.say("compact", f"~{size} tokens is over the limit ({self.cfg.context_limit}); "
                                    f"now ~{estimate_tokens(messages)}")
                self.trace.event("compact", before=size, after=estimate_tokens(messages))
            try:
                reply = with_retries(lambda: self.model.call(SYSTEM_PROMPT, messages, self.specs),
                                     on_retry=self.on_retry)
                self.breaker.record(ok=True)
            except TransientError as e:
                self.breaker.record(ok=False)
                self.say("error", f"model call failed ({e}); breaker at "
                                  f"{self.breaker.failures} of {self.breaker.threshold}")
                self.trace.event("model_error", error=str(e))
                if self.breaker.is_open:
                    return self.finish("stopped", "the model API keeps failing; the circuit breaker is open")
                continue
            tokens += reply.usage.get("input_tokens", 0) + reply.usage.get("output_tokens", 0)
            self.trace.event("model_call", turn=turn, stop_reason=reply.stop_reason, **reply.usage)
            messages.append({"role": "assistant", "content": reply.content})
            self.say(f"turn {turn}", f"model: {reply.text}" if reply.text else "model asks for tools")
            if reply.stop_reason == "max_tokens" and not reply.tool_calls:
                self.say("cut", "the reply was cut off; asking the model to continue")
                messages.append({"role": "user", "content": "You were cut off. Continue exactly where you stopped."})
                continue
            if not reply.tool_calls:
                return self.finish("done", f"after {turn} model calls", reply.text)
            results = []
            for call in reply.tool_calls:
                self.say("", f"tool   {call.name} {json.dumps(call.input)}")
                output, is_error = self.run_tool(call)
                self.trace.event("tool_call", tool=call.name, error=is_error, chars=len(output))
                self.say("", ("error  " if is_error else "result ") + one_line(output))
                results.append({"type": "tool_result", "tool_use_id": call.id,
                                "content": output, "is_error": is_error})
            messages.append({"role": "user", "content": results})
        return self.finish("stopped", f"turn limit reached ({self.cfg.max_turns} model calls)")

    def run_tool(self, call):
        """Run one tool call. Any error becomes a message for the model, never a crash."""
        tool = self.tools.get(call.name)
        if tool is None:
            return f"Unknown tool '{call.name}'. Available tools: {', '.join(self.tools)}.", True
        if self.loops.repeated(call):
            return "You made this exact call 3 times in a row. It is not helping. Try something else.", True
        verdict = self.check_permission(tool, call)
        self.say("", f"{verdict.decision:<6} {verdict.reason}")
        if verdict.decision == "deny":
            return f"Permission denied: {verdict.reason}. Do not retry this; find another way.", True
        try:
            output = tool.run(**call.input)
        except ToolError as e:
            return str(e), True
        except Exception as e:  # a bug, or an argument the model made up
            return f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}", True
        if len(output) > self.cfg.tool_output_chars:
            self.say("", f"cut    {len(output)} characters down to {self.cfg.tool_output_chars}")
        return truncate(output, self.cfg.tool_output_chars), False

    def check_permission(self, tool, call):
        key = call.name + json.dumps(call.input, sort_keys=True)
        if key in self.denied:
            verdict = Verdict("deny", "this exact call was already denied")
        else:
            verdict = decide(tool, call.input, self.cfg.mode)
        if verdict.decision == "ask":
            yes = ask_human(call, verdict.reason)
            verdict = Verdict("allow", "a human said yes") if yes else Verdict("deny", "a human said no")
        if verdict.decision == "deny":
            self.denied.add(key)
        self.trace.event("permission", tool=call.name, decision=verdict.decision, reason=verdict.reason)
        return verdict


# ============ COMMAND LINE ============

def main():
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Rein: a small coding-agent harness")
    p.add_argument("task", help="what you want done, in plain words")
    p.add_argument("--fake", metavar="SCENARIO", help="use a scripted model from this JSON file")
    p.add_argument("--workspace", default="demo", help="the project folder the agent works in")
    p.add_argument("--mode", default="ask", choices=["ask", "auto"], help="auto: allow edits without asking")
    p.add_argument("--context-limit", type=int, default=20000, help="compact above this many tokens")
    p.add_argument("--budget", type=int, default=200000, help="stop after spending this many tokens")
    args = p.parse_args()

    if args.fake:
        with open(args.fake) as f:
            scenario = json.load(f)
        model = FakeModel(scenario["replies"])
    else:
        model = ClaudeModel()

    cfg = Config(
        workspace=args.workspace,
        mode=args.mode,
        context_limit=args.context_limit,
        token_budget=args.budget,
    )
    trace = Tracer(cfg.trace_file)
    Agent(model, cfg, trace).run(args.task)
    show("trace", f"{trace.summary()} (details in {cfg.trace_file})")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

Carry this forward

  • Every automatic recovery is counted, limited, and able to give up.
  • Retry only what can succeed if you wait. A bug is not transient.
  • Backoff with jitter for hiccups; a circuit breaker for outages.
  • Guards: loop detection, continuing cut-off replies, a token budget, clean interrupts.

Steps 9 and 10: proof, then rules

Rein stops taking the model's word for it: when the model says "done", the harness runs a check, and a failed check becomes the next message. Then Rein learns to read the project's own rules.

9 The verification gate

GoalWhen the model says it is done, run a check. If the check fails, send the failure back and keep going. TheoryChapter 16: verification, done is a claim Sizerein.py grows to 523 lines

9aThe gate in the loop

Two new settings: check, the command that proves the work, and max_fix_rounds, how many times the gate may send the work back. When a reply has no tool calls, the model is claiming it is done. Instead of believing it, the loop now calls verify():

  • If the check passes, the run is done.
  • If it fails, Rein sends the failure output back as the next message, and the loop carries on.
  • If it still fails after the last fix round, the run ends as unverified. Never as done.

9bThe check itself

verify() runs the check command in the project folder, in the same clean environment as the tools, and records the result in the trace. The exit code decides: 0 means pass, anything else means fail. This is a Computational check: a program decides, not a model.

What just happened

Turn 3 claimed "Done!", but add() now multiplied. Rein ran python test_calc.py itself, saw it fail, and sent the failure back with the real output: add(2, 3) returned 6, expected 5. The model fixed it, ran the test itself this time, and claimed done again. Rein checked again, the check passed, and only then did the run end as done.

Compare this with step 4, when you answered n: a false claim of success went straight through. Now the check decides when the work is done, not the model's words.

Talking point: "Done is a claim. In my harness the model cannot finish a run while the check fails: the harness runs the check itself and sends failures back as the next message."

Break it on purpose

  1. Run the checkpoint without --check. The run ends at turn 3, with the wrong fix reported as done. Then run python demo/test_calc.py yourself.
  2. Give Rein a check that can never pass: --check "python test_nothing.py". After two fix rounds the run ends as unverified. The script runs out along the way, and that is fine: the gate still holds.
The whole rein.py after step 9 (523 lines)
"""Rein: a small coding-agent harness, built one step at a time.

Each section below is one system of the harness. Read it from top to bottom.
"""
import argparse
import json
import os
import random
import re
import subprocess
import time
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path


def show(tag, text):
    """Print one line of the live transcript: line breaks become spaces, and it is cut to fit."""
    print(f"{tag:<8} {' '.join(str(text).splitlines())}"[:100])


def one_line(text):
    """Join the lines of a tool result with ' | ' for the transcript. The model still gets all of it."""
    return " | ".join(line.strip() for line in str(text).splitlines() if line.strip())


# ============ MODEL: one interface, two models ============

class TransientError(Exception):
    """A failure worth retrying: overloaded, rate-limited, or a network blip."""


@dataclass
class ToolCall:
    id: str      # every request gets an id, so its result can be matched to it
    name: str    # which tool the model wants
    input: dict  # the arguments the model chose


@dataclass
class Reply:
    text: str          # what the model wrote
    tool_calls: list   # the tools it asked for (maybe none)
    stop_reason: str   # why it stopped: "end_turn", "tool_use", "max_tokens", ...
    content: list      # the raw blocks, saved into the history exactly as they came
    usage: dict = field(default_factory=dict)  # input and output token counts


class FakeModel:
    """Replays a script of replies, so every run is free, offline, and repeatable."""

    def __init__(self, replies):
        self.replies = list(replies)
        self.calls = 0

    def call(self, system, messages, tools):
        step = self.replies.pop(0) if self.replies else {"say": "(the script has run out)"}
        if "error" in step:            # a script can pretend the API failed
            if step.get("forever"):     # an outage that never ends
                self.replies.insert(0, step)
            raise TransientError(step["error"])
        content, tool_calls = [], []
        if step.get("say"):
            content.append({"type": "text", "text": step["say"]})
        for name, args in step.get("tools", []):
            self.calls += 1
            call = ToolCall(f"call_{self.calls}", name, args)
            tool_calls.append(call)
            content.append({"type": "tool_use", "id": call.id, "name": name, "input": args})
        stop = step.get("stop") or ("tool_use" if tool_calls else "end_turn")
        usage = {"input_tokens": len(json.dumps(messages)) // 4,
                 "output_tokens": len(json.dumps(content)) // 4}
        return Reply(step.get("say", ""), tool_calls, stop, content, usage)


class ClaudeModel:
    """The real model, behind exactly the same interface."""

    def __init__(self, model=None):
        import anthropic
        self.client = anthropic.Anthropic(max_retries=0)  # the harness owns retries now
        self.model = model or os.environ.get("REIN_MODEL", "claude-sonnet-5")
        names = ("RateLimitError", "APIConnectionError", "InternalServerError", "OverloadedError")
        self.retryable = tuple(getattr(anthropic, n) for n in names if hasattr(anthropic, n))

    def call(self, system, messages, tools):
        extra = {"tools": tools} if tools else {}
        try:
            r = self.client.messages.create(model=self.model, max_tokens=4096,
                                            system=system, messages=messages, **extra)
        except self.retryable as e:
            raise TransientError(type(e).__name__) from e
        content = [block.model_dump(exclude_none=True) for block in r.content]
        return Reply(
            text="".join(b["text"] for b in content if b["type"] == "text"),
            tool_calls=[ToolCall(b["id"], b["name"], b["input"])
                        for b in content if b["type"] == "tool_use"],
            stop_reason=r.stop_reason,
            content=content,
            usage={"input_tokens": r.usage.input_tokens,
                   "output_tokens": r.usage.output_tokens},
        )


# ============ TOOLS: the model's hands ============

class ToolError(Exception):
    """An expected failure. Its message goes back to the model, so write it for the model."""


class Workspace:
    """Every path goes through here, so the agent cannot leave its project folder."""

    def __init__(self, root):
        self.root = Path(root).resolve()

    def path(self, rel):
        p = (self.root / rel).resolve()
        if p != self.root and self.root not in p.parents:
            raise ToolError(f"'{rel}' is outside the project. Use a path inside it.")
        return p


@dataclass
class Tool:
    name: str
    description: str         # the model reads this, so write it for the model
    schema: dict             # the inputs, described as JSON Schema
    run: object              # the Python function the harness calls
    read_only: bool = False  # True if the tool never changes anything

    def spec(self):
        return {"name": self.name, "description": self.description, "input_schema": self.schema}


# Commands run without writing .pyc caches, so a check never runs stale code.
CLEAN_ENV = {**os.environ, "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE": "1"}


def make_tools(ws):
    """Four tools. Every error message tells the model what to do next."""

    def list_files():
        files = [p.relative_to(ws.root).as_posix() for p in sorted(ws.root.rglob("*")) if p.is_file()]
        return "\n".join(f for f in files if "__pycache__" not in f) or "(no files)"

    def read_file(path, start_line=1):
        p = ws.path(path)
        if not p.is_file():
            raise ToolError(f"No file at '{path}'. Call list_files to see what exists.")
        lines = p.read_text().splitlines()
        page = lines[start_line - 1:start_line + 199]
        text = "\n".join(f"{n}| {line}" for n, line in enumerate(page, start_line))
        last = start_line + len(page) - 1
        if last < len(lines):
            text += f"\n[lines {start_line}-{last} of {len(lines)}. Call again with start_line={last + 1}.]"
        return text or "(empty file)"

    def edit_file(path, old_text, new_text):
        p = ws.path(path)
        if not p.is_file():
            raise ToolError(f"No file at '{path}'. Call list_files to see what exists.")
        text = p.read_text()
        count = text.count(old_text)
        if count == 0:
            raise ToolError("old_text was not found. Read the file again: spaces must match exactly.")
        if count > 1:
            raise ToolError(f"old_text appears {count} times. Include more lines so it is unique.")
        p.write_text(text.replace(old_text, new_text))
        return f"Edited {path}."

    def run_command(command):
        try:
            r = subprocess.run(command, shell=True, cwd=ws.root, capture_output=True,
                               text=True, timeout=60, env=CLEAN_ENV)
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
            raise ToolError("Timed out after 60 seconds. Try a smaller command.")
        return f"exit code {r.returncode}\n{(r.stdout + r.stderr).strip() or '(no output)'}"

    string = {"type": "string"}
    tools = [
        Tool("list_files", "List every file in the project. Start here.",
             {"type": "object", "properties": {}}, list_files, read_only=True),
        Tool("read_file", "Read a text file with line numbers, 200 lines per page.",
             {"type": "object", "properties": {"path": string, "start_line": {"type": "integer"}},
              "required": ["path"]}, read_file, read_only=True),
        Tool("edit_file", "Replace one exact, unique piece of text in a file. Read the file first.",
             {"type": "object", "properties": {"path": string, "old_text": string, "new_text": string},
              "required": ["path", "old_text", "new_text"]}, edit_file),
        Tool("run_command", "Run a shell command in the project folder, for example the tests.",
             {"type": "object", "properties": {"command": string}, "required": ["command"]},
             run_command),
    ]
    return {t.name: t for t in tools}


# ============ PERMISSIONS: allow, ask, or deny ============

BLOCKED = [r"\brm\s", r"\bsudo\b", r"\bgit\s+push\b", r"\bcurl\b", r"\bwget\b"]  # never
ALLOWED = [r"^python\s+test_\w+\.py\b", r"^python\s+-m\s+pytest\b"]            # always fine
CHAINING = re.compile(r"[;&|`$<>]")  # an allowed command must not smuggle in a second one


@dataclass
class Verdict:
    decision: str  # "allow", "ask", or "deny"
    reason: str    # the model reads this too, so say why


def decide(tool, args, mode):
    """The policy. Deny rules are checked before allow rules."""
    if tool.read_only:
        return Verdict("allow", "read-only tool")
    if tool.name == "run_command":
        command = args.get("command", "").strip()
        if any(re.search(p, command) for p in BLOCKED):
            return Verdict("deny", "destructive, network, or admin command")
        if any(re.search(p, command) for p in ALLOWED) and not CHAINING.search(command):
            return Verdict("allow", "allow-listed command")
        return Verdict("ask", "this command is not on the allow-list")
    if mode == "auto":
        return Verdict("allow", "edits are allowed in auto mode")
    return Verdict("ask", "this tool changes files")


def ask_human(call, reason):
    answer = input(f"         ? allow {call.name}? ({reason}) [y/N] ")
    return answer.strip().lower() == "y"


# ============ TRACE: a record of everything ============

class Tracer:
    """Writes one JSON object per event to a file (JSONL), and keeps them for a summary."""

    def __init__(self, path):
        self.path, self.start, self.events = path, time.time(), []
        Path(path).write_text("")  # a fresh trace file for every run

    def event(self, kind, **data):
        record = {"t": round(time.time() - self.start, 3), "kind": kind, **data}
        self.events.append(record)
        with open(self.path, "a") as f:
            f.write(json.dumps(record) + "\n")

    def summary(self):
        counts = {}
        for e in self.events:
            if e["kind"] not in ("run_start", "run_end"):
                counts[e["kind"]] = counts.get(e["kind"], 0) + 1
        tokens = sum(e.get("input_tokens", 0) + e.get("output_tokens", 0) for e in self.events)
        return ", ".join(f"{n} {kind}" for kind, n in counts.items()) + f", {tokens} tokens"


# ============ CONTEXT: keep the desk clear ============

def estimate_tokens(messages):
    """About 4 characters per token. Rough, but good enough to decide when to compact."""
    return len(json.dumps(messages)) // 4


def truncate(text, max_chars):
    """Keep the head and the tail. In logs, the error is usually near the end."""
    if len(text) <= max_chars:
        return text
    half = max_chars // 2
    return f"{text[:half]}\n[... {len(text) - max_chars} characters cut by the harness ...]\n{text[-half:]}"


def compact(messages, task, keep_last=4):
    """Swap old history for a short summary. Keep the newest messages word for word."""
    cut = len(messages) - keep_last
    while cut > 1 and messages[cut]["role"] != "assistant":  # never split a tool call from its result
        cut -= 1
    if cut <= 1:
        return None  # nothing old enough to summarize yet
    used = [b["name"] for m in messages[:cut] if isinstance(m["content"], list)
            for b in m["content"] if b.get("type") == "tool_use"]
    summary = ("[The harness summarized earlier work to save space.]\n"
               f"Original task: {task}\n"
               f"Tools used so far: {', '.join(used) or 'none'}\n"
               "Re-read any file before you edit it. Then continue the task.")
    return [{"role": "user", "content": summary}] + messages[cut:]


# ============ RECOVERY: counted, limited, able to give up ============

def with_retries(call, on_retry, attempts=3, base=0.5, cap=8.0):
    """Try again after a transient failure, waiting longer each time."""
    for attempt in range(1, attempts + 1):
        try:
            return call()
        except TransientError as e:
            if attempt == attempts:
                raise                                    # out of attempts: the caller decides
            delay = min(cap, base * 2 ** (attempt - 1))  # 0.5s, 1s, 2s, 4s... never above cap
            on_retry(attempt, delay, e)
            time.sleep(delay * random.uniform(0.5, 1.0))  # jitter: never retry in lockstep


class CircuitBreaker:
    """After `threshold` failed calls in a row, stop trying and say so clearly."""

    def __init__(self, threshold=3):
        self.threshold, self.failures = threshold, 0

    def record(self, ok):
        self.failures = 0 if ok else self.failures + 1

    @property
    def is_open(self):
        return self.failures >= self.threshold


class LoopDetector:
    """Notices the exact same tool call, made several times in a row."""

    def __init__(self, limit=3):
        self.limit, self.last, self.count = limit, None, 0

    def repeated(self, call):
        key = call.name + json.dumps(call.input, sort_keys=True)
        self.count = self.count + 1 if key == self.last else 1
        self.last = key
        return self.count >= self.limit


def close_open_calls(messages):
    """After an interrupt, every tool request still needs a result, or the history is broken."""
    last = messages[-1]
    if last["role"] == "assistant":
        ids = [b["id"] for b in last["content"] if b.get("type") == "tool_use"]
        if ids:
            messages.append({"role": "user", "content": [
                {"type": "tool_result", "tool_use_id": i, "content": "Interrupted before it ran.",
                 "is_error": True} for i in ids]})


# ============ THE LOOP ============

SYSTEM_PROMPT = ("You are Rein, a careful coding agent working inside one project folder. "
                 "Look before you change anything, and run the tests to prove your work.")


@dataclass
class Config:
    max_turns: int = 10  # hard stop: the most model calls one run may use
    workspace: str = "demo"  # the only folder the agent may touch
    mode: str = "ask"        # "ask": edits need a human yes. "auto": edits are allowed
    trace_file: str = "trace.jsonl"  # every event of the run is recorded here
    tool_output_chars: int = 2000    # cap on each tool result that enters the context
    context_limit: int = 20000       # estimated tokens before old history is compacted
    token_budget: int = 200000       # hard stop: the most tokens one run may spend
    check: str = ""                  # a command that proves the work, like "python test_calc.py"
    max_fix_rounds: int = 2          # how many times the gate may send work back


class Agent:
    def __init__(self, model, cfg, trace):
        self.model, self.cfg, self.trace = model, cfg, trace
        self.tools = make_tools(Workspace(cfg.workspace))
        self.specs = [t.spec() for t in self.tools.values()]
        self.denied = set()  # once denied, always denied
        self.breaker = CircuitBreaker()
        self.loops = LoopDetector()

    def say(self, tag, text):
        show(tag, text)

    def finish(self, status, note, answer=""):
        """Every way a run can end goes through here, so none of them gets forgotten."""
        self.say(status, note)
        self.trace.event("run_end", status=status)
        return answer

    def on_retry(self, attempt, delay, error):
        self.say("retry", f"attempt {attempt} failed ({error}); waiting up to {delay:.1f}s")
        self.trace.event("retry", attempt=attempt, error=str(error))

    def run(self, task):
        messages = [{"role": "user", "content": task}]
        try:
            return self.loop(task, messages)
        except KeyboardInterrupt:  # Ctrl+C: stop cleanly and leave a valid history behind
            close_open_calls(messages)
            return self.finish("stopped", f"interrupted; history left valid ({len(messages)} messages)")

    def loop(self, task, messages):
        self.say("task", task)
        self.trace.event("run_start", task=task)
        tokens = fix_rounds = 0
        for turn in range(1, self.cfg.max_turns + 1):
            if tokens >= self.cfg.token_budget:
                return self.finish("stopped", f"token budget spent ({tokens} of {self.cfg.token_budget})")
            size = estimate_tokens(messages)
            smaller = compact(messages, task) if size > self.cfg.context_limit else None
            if smaller:
                messages[:] = smaller  # change the list in place, so every holder sees it
                self.say("compact", f"~{size} tokens is over the limit ({self.cfg.context_limit}); "
                                    f"now ~{estimate_tokens(messages)}")
                self.trace.event("compact", before=size, after=estimate_tokens(messages))
            try:
                reply = with_retries(lambda: self.model.call(SYSTEM_PROMPT, messages, self.specs),
                                     on_retry=self.on_retry)
                self.breaker.record(ok=True)
            except TransientError as e:
                self.breaker.record(ok=False)
                self.say("error", f"model call failed ({e}); breaker at "
                                  f"{self.breaker.failures} of {self.breaker.threshold}")
                self.trace.event("model_error", error=str(e))
                if self.breaker.is_open:
                    return self.finish("stopped", "the model API keeps failing; the circuit breaker is open")
                continue
            tokens += reply.usage.get("input_tokens", 0) + reply.usage.get("output_tokens", 0)
            self.trace.event("model_call", turn=turn, stop_reason=reply.stop_reason, **reply.usage)
            messages.append({"role": "assistant", "content": reply.content})
            self.say(f"turn {turn}", f"model: {reply.text}" if reply.text else "model asks for tools")
            if reply.stop_reason == "max_tokens" and not reply.tool_calls:
                self.say("cut", "the reply was cut off; asking the model to continue")
                messages.append({"role": "user", "content": "You were cut off. Continue exactly where you stopped."})
                continue
            if not reply.tool_calls:  # the model says it is done. That is a claim: check it.
                passed, report = self.verify()
                if passed:
                    return self.finish("done", f"after {turn} model calls", reply.text)
                if fix_rounds == self.cfg.max_fix_rounds:
                    return self.finish("unverified", f"still failing after {fix_rounds} fix rounds", reply.text)
                fix_rounds += 1
                self.say("check", f"not done: {one_line(report)}")
                messages.append({"role": "user", "content": "The harness checked your work and it is not done "
                                 f"yet:\n{report}\nFix the problem, and check it yourself before you finish."})
                continue
            results = []
            for call in reply.tool_calls:
                self.say("", f"tool   {call.name} {json.dumps(call.input)}")
                output, is_error = self.run_tool(call)
                self.trace.event("tool_call", tool=call.name, error=is_error, chars=len(output))
                self.say("", ("error  " if is_error else "result ") + one_line(output))
                results.append({"type": "tool_result", "tool_use_id": call.id,
                                "content": output, "is_error": is_error})
            messages.append({"role": "user", "content": results})
        return self.finish("stopped", f"turn limit reached ({self.cfg.max_turns} model calls)")

    def run_tool(self, call):
        """Run one tool call. Any error becomes a message for the model, never a crash."""
        tool = self.tools.get(call.name)
        if tool is None:
            return f"Unknown tool '{call.name}'. Available tools: {', '.join(self.tools)}.", True
        if self.loops.repeated(call):
            return "You made this exact call 3 times in a row. It is not helping. Try something else.", True
        verdict = self.check_permission(tool, call)
        self.say("", f"{verdict.decision:<6} {verdict.reason}")
        if verdict.decision == "deny":
            return f"Permission denied: {verdict.reason}. Do not retry this; find another way.", True
        try:
            output = tool.run(**call.input)
        except ToolError as e:
            return str(e), True
        except Exception as e:  # a bug, or an argument the model made up
            return f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}", True
        if len(output) > self.cfg.tool_output_chars:
            self.say("", f"cut    {len(output)} characters down to {self.cfg.tool_output_chars}")
        return truncate(output, self.cfg.tool_output_chars), False

    def verify(self):
        """'Done' is a claim. Run the check command and see for ourselves."""
        if self.cfg.check:
            r = subprocess.run(self.cfg.check, shell=True, cwd=self.cfg.workspace, capture_output=True,
                               text=True, timeout=300, env=CLEAN_ENV)
            self.trace.event("verify", check=self.cfg.check, passed=r.returncode == 0)
            if r.returncode != 0:
                return False, (r.stdout + r.stderr).strip()
            self.say("check", f"passed: {self.cfg.check}")
        return True, "ok"

    def check_permission(self, tool, call):
        key = call.name + json.dumps(call.input, sort_keys=True)
        if key in self.denied:
            verdict = Verdict("deny", "this exact call was already denied")
        else:
            verdict = decide(tool, call.input, self.cfg.mode)
        if verdict.decision == "ask":
            yes = ask_human(call, verdict.reason)
            verdict = Verdict("allow", "a human said yes") if yes else Verdict("deny", "a human said no")
        if verdict.decision == "deny":
            self.denied.add(key)
        self.trace.event("permission", tool=call.name, decision=verdict.decision, reason=verdict.reason)
        return verdict


# ============ COMMAND LINE ============

def main():
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Rein: a small coding-agent harness")
    p.add_argument("task", help="what you want done, in plain words")
    p.add_argument("--fake", metavar="SCENARIO", help="use a scripted model from this JSON file")
    p.add_argument("--workspace", default="demo", help="the project folder the agent works in")
    p.add_argument("--mode", default="ask", choices=["ask", "auto"], help="auto: allow edits without asking")
    p.add_argument("--context-limit", type=int, default=20000, help="compact above this many tokens")
    p.add_argument("--budget", type=int, default=200000, help="stop after spending this many tokens")
    p.add_argument("--check", default="", help='a command that proves the work, e.g. "python test_calc.py"')
    args = p.parse_args()

    if args.fake:
        with open(args.fake) as f:
            scenario = json.load(f)
        model = FakeModel(scenario["replies"])
    else:
        model = ClaudeModel()

    cfg = Config(
        workspace=args.workspace,
        mode=args.mode,
        context_limit=args.context_limit,
        token_budget=args.budget,
        check=args.check,
    )
    trace = Tracer(cfg.trace_file)
    Agent(model, cfg, trace).run(args.task)
    show("trace", f"{trace.summary()} (details in {cfg.trace_file})")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

10 Project rules

GoalBuild the system prompt from layers: Rein's own rules first, then the project's AGENTS.md. TheoryChapter 9: control plane, instructions as law Sizerein.py grows to 544 lines

10aLayers

The single prompt string becomes two layers. BASE_PROMPT holds Rein's own rules, numbered, most important first. build_system_prompt() adds the project's AGENTS.md if there is one, and says which layer wins when they disagree. That order is called Precedence.

The prompt is built once per run, not on every turn. Sending the exact same prompt on every call is what lets Prompt caching work, which makes calls cheaper and faster (chapter 14).

new code (replaces the red lines)
